\section*{How to use this packet}
For each source claim, compare the exact source excerpts with the expanded
PaperInterface specification. Mark up this PDF or fill the blank reviewer box in the TeX source;
return the annotated file for reconciliation into the paper-local human-review
log.

\section*{Open the interactive dashboard}
The interactive dashboard is an optional alternative to using this PDF; it
presents the same review surface rather than an additional review requirement.
After forking and cloning (or pulling) AppliedModelingLib, run the following from the
repository root. The cache command is needed only the first time in a new clone.
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
cd <your-repository-checkout>
lake exe cache get
python3 scripts/review_dashboard.py --paper LG21TestOptionalPolicies --serve
\end{ReviewVerbatim}
Open \url{http://127.0.0.1:8765/} in a browser and keep that terminal running
while reviewing. The dashboard records saved annotations in this paper's local
reviewer-owned review record.

type:
{α : Type u_1} → [inst : MeasurableSpace α] → (mean : α → ℝ) → Measurable mean → NNReal → ProbabilityTheory.Kernel α ℝ

fun {α : Type u_1} [MeasurableSpace α] (mean : α → ℝ) (hmean : Measurable mean) (variance : NNReal) =>
  ((ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.deterministic mean hmean).prod

    fun (pair : ℝ × ℝ) => pair.1 + pair.2
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

\textbf{Declaration location:} paper-local
\paragraph{Lean declaration body}
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
type:
{Omega : Type u_1} →
  {Base : Type u_2} →
    {Estimate : Type u_3} →
      [inst : MeasurableSpace Omega] →
        [inst_1 : MeasurableSpace Base] →
          [inst_2 : MeasurableSpace Estimate] →
            [StandardBorelSpace Estimate] →
              [Nonempty Estimate] →
                MeasureTheory.Measure Base →
                  (Omega → Base) →
                    (accessLaw noAccessLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure Omega) →
                      [MeasureTheory.IsFiniteMeasure accessLaw] →
                        [MeasureTheory.IsFiniteMeasure noAccessLaw] → (Omega → Estimate) → Prop

value:
fun {Omega : Type u_1} {Base : Type u_2} {Estimate : Type u_3} [MeasurableSpace Omega] [MeasurableSpace Base]
    [MeasurableSpace Estimate] [StandardBorelSpace Estimate] [Nonempty Estimate] (baseLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure Base)
    (base : Omega → Base) (accessLaw noAccessLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure Omega)
    [MeasureTheory.IsFiniteMeasure accessLaw] [MeasureTheory.IsFiniteMeasure noAccessLaw] (output : Omega → Estimate) =>
  MeasureTheory.Measure.map base accessLaw = baseLaw ∧
    MeasureTheory.Measure.map base noAccessLaw = baseLaw ∧
      ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib output base accessLaw) =ᵐ[baseLaw]
        ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib output base noAccessLaw)
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

\textbf{Declaration location:} paper-local
\paragraph{Lean declaration body}
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
type:
LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21RequirementPolicy → LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21AccessAction → Prop

value:
fun (a : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21RequirementPolicy) (a_1 : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21AccessAction) =>
  (match (motive := LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21RequirementPolicy → LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21AccessAction → Prop)
      a with
    | LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21RequirementPolicy.noRequirements =>
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21AccessAction.optionalReportingRequirement
    | LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21RequirementPolicy.reportRequiredConditionalTaking =>
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21AccessAction.reportRequiredAfterTaking
    | LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21RequirementPolicy.reportRequiredGivenAccess =>
      fun (action : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21AccessAction) =>
      action = LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21AccessAction.takeAndReport)
    a_1
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

value:
fun {Feature : Type u_1} [Fintype Feature] [DecidableEq Feature]
    (M : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ContinuousGaussianPopulation Feature) (haccess : 0 < M.accessLaw {true})
    (hnoAccess : 0 < M.accessLaw {false}) (testFeature : Feature)
    (mandatory : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21MandatoryGivenAccessLiteralSourceEquilibrium M)
    (reportedPayoff : (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ)
    (noAccessOutput : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ) → ℝ) =>
  (∀ (student : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)),
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21RequirementPolicy.reportRequiredGivenAccess.feasibleAction
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationAccessStatus student) (mandatory.action student)) ∧
    (∀ (student : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)),
        mandatory.action student =
          if LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationAccess student = true then
            LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21AccessAction.takeAndReport
          else LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21AccessAction.noTake) ∧
      (∀ᵐ (student : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) ∂LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianAccessPopulationLaw M,
          mandatory.action student = LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21AccessAction.takeAndReport) ∧
        LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ObservedAccessAllReportPBO
            (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianAccessPopulationLaw M)
            (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationBase testFeature)
            (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationFeature testFeature)
            LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationSkill reportedPayoff ∧
          LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ContinuousGaussianNoAccessPopulationPBO M hnoAccess testFeature noAccessOutput
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

fun {Feature : Type u_1} [Fintype Feature] [DecidableEq Feature]
    (M : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ContinuousGaussianPopulation Feature) (testFeature : Feature)
    (region : Set (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ))
    (hregionPositive :
      0 <
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianPopulationLaw M)
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessBaseRegionEvent testFeature region))
    (candidateTake : ℝ → (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) → Bool)
    (candidateReport : (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) → ℝ → Bool)
    (candidate :
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalCandidateBranchData ℝ
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) ℝ)
    (hnoReportPositive :
      0 <
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessLocalRawLaw M testFeature region)
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessRawCandidateNoReportEvent testFeature candidateTake
            candidateReport)) =>

    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianPopulationLaw M;
  let localLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure (Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) :=
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessLocalRawLaw M testFeature region;

  let actionLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure ((LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) × ℝ) :=

value:
fun {Feature : Type u_1} [Fintype Feature] [DecidableEq Feature]
    (M : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ContinuousGaussianPopulation Feature) (testFeature : Feature)
    (region : Set (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ))
    (hregionPositive :
      0 <
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianPopulationLaw M)
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessBaseRegionEvent testFeature region))
    (candidateTake : ℝ → (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) → Bool)
    (candidateReport : (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) → ℝ → Bool)
    (candidate :
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalCandidateBranchData ℝ
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) ℝ)
    (hreportPositive :
      0 <
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessLocalRawLaw M testFeature region)
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessRawCandidateReportEvent testFeature candidateTake
            candidateReport)) =>
  have rawLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure (Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) :=
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianPopulationLaw M;
  let localLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure (Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) :=
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessLocalRawLaw M testFeature region;
  let reportEvent : Set (Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) :=
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessRawCandidateReportEvent testFeature candidateTake candidateReport;
  let actionLaw :
    MeasureTheory.Measure ((LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) × ℝ × ℝ) :=
    MeasureTheory.Measure.map (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessBaseScoreSkillObservation testFeature)
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21NormalizedRestriction localLaw reportEvent);
  ∀ᵐ (publicObservation :
    (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) ×
      ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.Measure.map
      (fun (scoreSkill : (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) × ℝ × ℝ) =>
        (scoreSkill.1, scoreSkill.2.1))
      actionLaw,
    candidate.reportedValue publicObservation.1 publicObservation.2 =
      ∫ (latentSkill : ℝ),
        latentSkill ∂(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib
            (fun (scoreSkill : (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) × ℝ × ℝ) =>
              scoreSkill.2.2)
            (fun (scoreSkill : (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) × ℝ × ℝ) =>
              (scoreSkill.1, scoreSkill.2.1))
            actionLaw)
          publicObservation
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

value:
fun {Feature : Type u_1} [Fintype Feature] [DecidableEq Feature]
    (M : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ContinuousGaussianPopulation Feature) (testFeature : Feature)
    (takeDecision : ℝ → (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) → Bool)
    (reportDecision : (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) → ℝ → Bool)
    (noReportPayoff : (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) → ℝ) =>
  0 <
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianPopulationLaw M)
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessOptionalNoReportEvent testFeature takeDecision reportDecision) →
    have law : MeasureTheory.Measure (Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) :=
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianPopulationLaw M;
    have noReportLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure (Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) :=
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessOptionalNoReportLaw M testFeature takeDecision reportDecision;
    have base : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ) → LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ :=
      fun (student : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) =>
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessStudentBase testFeature student.2;
    (fun (student : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) => noReportPayoff (base student)) =ᵐ[noReportLaw]
      noReportLaw[LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationSkill | MeasurableSpace.comap base inferInstance]
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

type:
{Feature : Type u_1} →
  [inst : Fintype Feature] →
    [inst_1 : DecidableEq Feature] →
      (source : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ObservedAccessGaussianSource Feature) →
        0 < source.population.accessLaw {false} →
          LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21P42MandatoryGivenAccessPBOProfile source →
            ((LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature source.test_feature → ℝ) → ℝ) → Prop

value:
fun {Feature : Type u_1} [Fintype Feature] [DecidableEq Feature]
    (source : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ObservedAccessGaussianSource Feature)
    (hnoAccess : 0 < source.population.accessLaw {false})
    (profile : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21P42MandatoryGivenAccessPBOProfile source)
    (noReportPayoff : (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature source.test_feature → ℝ) → ℝ) =>
  ∃ (baseLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature source.test_feature → ℝ))
    (baseMean : (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature source.test_feature → ℝ) → ℝ) (baseVariance : ℝ)
    (hbaseMean : Measurable baseMean) (hbaseLaw : MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure baseLaw) (hbaseVariance :
    0 < baseVariance),
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianFullBaseLatentPrimitiveLaw source.population source.test_feature =
        baseLaw.compProd
          (AppliedModelingLib.Probability.gaussianLocationKernel baseMean hbaseMean baseVariance.toNNReal) ∧
      let S :
        LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21GaussianPBOResamplingSource
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature source.test_feature → ℝ) :=
        { baseLaw := baseLaw,
          baseLaw_isProbability :=
            LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21Definition6ObservedAccessSourceWitness._proof_1 source baseLaw hbaseLaw,
          posteriorBaseMean := baseMean, posteriorBaseMean_measurable := hbaseMean,
          posteriorBaseVariance := baseVariance.toNNReal,
          posteriorBaseVariance_pos :=
            LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21Definition6ObservedAccessSourceWitness._proof_2 baseVariance hbaseVariance,
          testNoiseVariance := source.population.noiseVariance source.test_feature,
          testNoiseVariance_pos := source.test_noise_variance_positive };
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ObservedAccessFair source.population source.test_feature
        {
          accessOutput :=
            LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ObservedAccessActualOutput
              (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationBase source.test_feature)
              (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationFeature source.test_feature) profile.mandatory.action
              profile.reportedPayoff noReportPayoff,
          noAccessKernel := LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21D6NoAccessResamplingEstimateKernel S,
          noAccessKernel_isMarkov :=
            LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21T44MandatoryGivenAccessResamplingFairnessCertificate._proof_1 source baseLaw
              baseMean baseVariance hbaseMean hbaseLaw hbaseVariance }
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

              (score skill : Omega → ℝ) →
                (Measurable fun (omega : Omega) => (base omega, score omega, skill omega)) →
                  (candidateTake : ℝ → Base → Bool) →
                    (candidateReport : Base → ℝ → Bool) →

value:
fun {Omega : Type u_1} {Base : Type u_2} [MeasurableSpace Omega] [MeasurableSpace Base]
    (sourceLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure Omega) [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure sourceLaw] (base : Omega → Base)
    (score skill : Omega → ℝ) (hpublic : Measurable fun (omega : Omega) => (base omega, score omega, skill omega))
    (candidateTake : ℝ → Base → Bool) (candidateReport : Base → ℝ → Bool)
    (candidate : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalCandidateBranchData ℝ Base ℝ)
    (hpositive :
      0 <
        sourceLaw
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalSourceNoReportEvent base score skill candidateTake candidateReport)) =>
  let actionLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure (Base × ℝ × ℝ) :=
    MeasureTheory.Measure.map (fun (omega : Omega) => (base omega, score omega, skill omega))
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21NormalizedRestriction sourceLaw
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalSourceNoReportEvent base score skill candidateTake candidateReport));
  ∀ᵐ (publicBase : Base) ∂MeasureTheory.Measure.map Prod.fst actionLaw,
    candidate.noReportValue publicBase =
      ∫ (scoreSkill : ℝ × ℝ), scoreSkill.2 ∂(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib Prod.snd Prod.fst actionLaw) publicBase
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

value:
fun {Omega : Type u_1} {Base : Type u_2} [MeasurableSpace Omega] [MeasurableSpace Base]
    (sourceLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure Omega) [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure sourceLaw] (base : Omega → Base)
    (score skill : Omega → ℝ) (hpublic : Measurable fun (omega : Omega) => (base omega, score omega, skill omega))
    (candidateTake : ℝ → Base → Bool) (candidateReport : Base → ℝ → Bool)
    (candidate : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalCandidateBranchData ℝ Base ℝ)
    (hpositive :
      0 <
        sourceLaw
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalSourceReportEvent base score skill candidateTake candidateReport)) =>
  let actionLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure (Base × ℝ × ℝ) :=
    MeasureTheory.Measure.map (fun (omega : Omega) => (base omega, score omega, skill omega))
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21NormalizedRestriction sourceLaw
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalSourceReportEvent base score skill candidateTake candidateReport));
  ∀ᵐ (publicObservation :
    Base × ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.Measure.map (fun (scoreSkill : Base × ℝ × ℝ) => (scoreSkill.1, scoreSkill.2.1)) actionLaw,
    candidate.reportedValue publicObservation.1 publicObservation.2 =
      ∫ (latentSkill : ℝ),
        latentSkill ∂(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib (fun (scoreSkill : Base × ℝ × ℝ) => scoreSkill.2.2)
            (fun (scoreSkill : Base × ℝ × ℝ) => (scoreSkill.1, scoreSkill.2.1)) actionLaw)
          publicObservation
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

type:

value:
fun {Omega : Type u_1} {Base : Type u_2} [MeasurableSpace Omega] [MeasurableSpace Base]
    (sourceLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure Omega) [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure sourceLaw] (base : Omega → Base)
    (score skill : Omega → ℝ) (hpublic : Measurable fun (omega : Omega) => (base omega, score omega, skill omega))
    (currentTake : ℝ → Base → Bool) (currentReport : Base → ℝ → Bool) (region : Set Base)
    (candidateTake : ℝ → Base → Bool) (candidateReport : Base → ℝ → Bool)
    (candidate : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalCandidateBranchData ℝ Base ℝ) =>
  MeasurableSet region ∧
    ∃ (hregionPositive : 0 < sourceLaw (base ⁻¹' region)),
      sourceLaw
            (base ⁻¹' region ∩
              LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalSourceReportEvent base score skill currentTake currentReport) =
          0 ∧
        (Measurable fun (omega : Omega) => candidateTake (skill omega) (base omega)) ∧
          (Measurable fun (omega : Omega) => candidateReport (base omega) (score omega)) ∧
            (∀ᵐ (omega : Omega) ∂sourceLaw,
                base omega ∉ region →
                  candidateTake (skill omega) (base omega) = currentTake (skill omega) (base omega) ∧
                    candidateReport (base omega) (score omega) = currentReport (base omega) (score omega)) ∧
              0 <
                  sourceLaw
                    (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalSourceChangedTesterEvent base skill currentTake
                        candidateTake ∪
                      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalSourceChangedToReportForActionEvent base score skill
                        currentTake currentReport candidateTake candidateReport) ∧
                (∀
                    omega ∈
                      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalSourceChangedTesterEvent base skill currentTake
                        candidateTake,
                    candidate.noReportValue (base omega) <
                      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalCandidateTestExpectedValue candidate (skill omega)
                        (base omega)) ∧
                  let localLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure Omega :=
                    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21NormalizedRestriction sourceLaw (base ⁻¹' region);
                  ((fun (omega : Omega) => candidateReport (base omega) (score omega)) =ᵐ[localLaw]
                      fun (omega : Omega) => candidate.reportDecision (base omega) (score omega)) ∧
                    ∃ (hreportPositive :
                      0 <
                        localLaw
                          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalSourceReportEvent base score skill candidateTake
                            candidateReport))
                      (hnoReportPositive :
                      0 <
                        localLaw
                          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalSourceNoReportEvent base score skill candidateTake
                            candidateReport)),
                      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalSequentialCandidateReportPBOForAction localLaw base score
                          skill hpublic candidateTake candidateReport candidate hreportPositive ∧
                        LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalSequentialCandidateNoReportPBOForAction localLaw base score
                            skill hpublic candidateTake candidateReport candidate hnoReportPositive ∧
                          LG21TestOptionalPolicies.PositiveMassBranchMembersBestRespond localLaw
                            (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalSourceReportEvent base score skill candidateTake
                              candidateReport)
                            candidate
                            fun (P : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalCandidateBranchData ℝ Base ℝ)
                              (omega : Omega) =>
                            P.noReportValue (base omega) ≤ P.reportedValue (base omega) (score omega)
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

type:

value:
fun {Omega : Type u_1} {Base : Type u_2} [MeasurableSpace Omega] [MeasurableSpace Base] (base : Omega → Base)
    (score skill : Omega → ℝ) (A : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalSourceTimedActions Base) (a : Omega) =>
  if A.takeDecision (skill a) (base a) = true ∧ A.reportDecision (base a) (score a) = true then
    A.reportedPayoff (base a) (score a)
  else A.noReportPayoff (base a)
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

  {M : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ContinuousGaussianPopulation Feature} {haccess : 0 < M.accessLaw {true}}
  {testFeature : Feature}
  (self : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalSourceTimedPositiveMassSelfEnforcingCandidate M haccess testFeature),
  0 <
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianAccessPopulationLaw M)
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalCandidateNoReportBranch
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationBase testFeature)
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationFeature testFeature)
          LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationSkill self.source_timed.actions) →
    ∀ᵐ (student :
      Bool ×
        ℝ ×
          (Feature →
            ℝ)) ∂(LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianAccessPopulationLaw M).restrict
        {student : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ) |
          self.source_timed.actions.takeDecision (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationSkill student)
              (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationBase testFeature student) =
            false},
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalSourceTimedBestContinuationExpectedPayoff self.source_timed.actions
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationSkill student)
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationBase testFeature student) ≤
        self.source_timed.actions.noReportPayoff
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationBase testFeature student)

          self.source_timed.actions.noReportPayoff
            (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationBase testFeature student)) ∧
      ∀ᵐ (student :
        Bool ×
          ℝ ×
            (Feature →

          {student : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ) |
            self.source_timed.actions.takeDecision (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationSkill student)
                (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationBase testFeature student) =

            (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationBase testFeature student) ≤
          self.source_timed.actions.noReportPayoff
            (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationBase testFeature student)

field selected_self_enforcing:
∀ {Feature : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype Feature] [inst_1 : DecidableEq Feature]
  {M : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ContinuousGaussianPopulation Feature} {haccess : 0 < M.accessLaw {true}}
  {testFeature : Feature}
  {selected : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ObservedAccessOptionalPositiveMassRefinedEquilibrium M haccess testFeature},
  LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalFibrewiseActiveBranchSelection M haccess testFeature selected →
    ∃ (candidate :
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalSourceTimedPositiveMassSelfEnforcingCandidate M haccess testFeature),
      candidate.source_timed = selected

field maximal_active_branch_ae:
∀ {Feature : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype Feature] [inst_1 : DecidableEq Feature]
  {M : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ContinuousGaussianPopulation Feature} {haccess : 0 < M.accessLaw {true}}
  {testFeature : Feature}
  {selected : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ObservedAccessOptionalPositiveMassRefinedEquilibrium M haccess testFeature},
  LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalFibrewiseActiveBranchSelection M haccess testFeature selected →
    let law : MeasureTheory.Measure (Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) :=
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianAccessPopulationLaw M;
    let base : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ) → LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ :=
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationBase testFeature;
    let score : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ) → ℝ := LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationFeature testFeature;
    let skill : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ) → ℝ := LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationSkill;
    ∀
      (candidate :
        LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalSourceTimedPositiveMassSelfEnforcingCandidate M haccess testFeature)
      (region : Set (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ)),
      MeasurableSet region →
        0 < law (base ⁻¹' region) →
          (∀ᵐ (student : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) ∂law.restrict (base ⁻¹' region),
              selected.actions.takeDecision (skill student) (base student) = true ∧
                  selected.actions.reportDecision (base student) (score student) = true →
                candidate.source_timed.actions.takeDecision (skill student) (base student) = true ∧
                  candidate.source_timed.actions.reportDecision (base student) (score student) = true) →
            ∀ᵐ (student : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) ∂law.restrict (base ⁻¹' region),
              candidate.source_timed.actions.takeDecision (skill student) (base student) = true ∧
                  candidate.source_timed.actions.reportDecision (base student) (score student) = true →
                selected.actions.takeDecision (skill student) (base student) = true ∧
                  selected.actions.reportDecision (base student) (score student) = true
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

type:
{Base : Type u_1} →
  [inst : MeasurableSpace Base] → LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21P42ObservedScoreGaussianPBOModel Base → Base → ℝ → ℝ

value:
fun {Base : Type u_1} [MeasurableSpace Base] (M : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21P42ObservedScoreGaussianPBOModel Base)
    (base : Base) (skill : ℝ) =>
  (M.basePosteriorMean base / ↑M.basePosteriorVariance + skill / ↑M.testNoiseVariance) /
    (1 / ↑M.basePosteriorVariance + 1 / ↑M.testNoiseVariance)
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

type:

value:
fun {Base : Type u_1} [MeasurableSpace Base] (M : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21P42ObservedScoreGaussianPBOModel Base) =>
  NNReal.mk
    ((1 / ↑M.basePosteriorVariance + 2 / ↑M.testNoiseVariance) /
      (1 / ↑M.basePosteriorVariance + 1 / ↑M.testNoiseVariance) ^ 2)
    (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21P42SourceDisplayedAccessEstimateVariance._proof_2 M)
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

        (sourceLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure Omega) →
          [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure sourceLaw] →
            (base : Omega → Base) →
              (score skill : Omega → ℝ) →
                (Measurable fun (omega : Omega) => (base omega, score omega, skill omega)) →
                  (candidate : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ReportRequiredSequentialEquilibriumData ℝ Base ℝ) →

                        sourceLaw
                          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ReportRequiredCandidateSourceNoTakeEvent base skill candidate) →

value:
fun {Omega : Type u_1} {Base : Type u_2} [MeasurableSpace Omega] [MeasurableSpace Base]
    (sourceLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure Omega) [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure sourceLaw] (base : Omega → Base)
    (score skill : Omega → ℝ) (hpublic : Measurable fun (omega : Omega) => (base omega, score omega, skill omega))
    (candidate : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ReportRequiredSequentialEquilibriumData ℝ Base ℝ)
    (hpositive :
      0 < sourceLaw (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ReportRequiredCandidateSourceNoTakeEvent base skill candidate)) =>
  let actionLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure (Base × ℝ × ℝ) :=
    MeasureTheory.Measure.map (fun (omega : Omega) => (base omega, score omega, skill omega))
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21NormalizedRestriction sourceLaw
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ReportRequiredCandidateSourceNoTakeEvent base skill candidate));
  ∀ᵐ (publicBase : Base) ∂MeasureTheory.Measure.map Prod.fst actionLaw,
    candidate.noReportPayoff publicBase =

fun {Omega : Type u_1} {Base : Type u_2} [MeasurableSpace Omega] [MeasurableSpace Base]
    (sourceLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure Omega) [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure sourceLaw] (base : Omega → Base)

    (candidate : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ReportRequiredSequentialEquilibriumData ℝ Base ℝ)

    MeasureTheory.Measure.map (fun (omega : Omega) => (base omega, score omega, skill omega))

\textbf{Declaration location:} paper-local

\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
field selected_self_enforcing:
∀ {Feature : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype Feature] [inst_1 : DecidableEq Feature]
  {M : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ContinuousGaussianPopulation Feature} {haccess : 0 < M.accessLaw {true}}
  {testFeature : Feature}
  {selected :
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ReportRequiredSequentialEquilibriumData ℝ
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) ℝ}
  {selectedSource :
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ReportRequiredPositiveMassRefinedSourceEquilibrium
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianAccessPopulationLaw M)
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationBase testFeature)
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationFeature testFeature)
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationSkill selected (M.noiseVariance testFeature)},
  LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ReportRequiredFibrewiseActiveBranchSelection M haccess testFeature selected
      selectedSource →
    ∃ (candidate :
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ReportRequiredSourceTimedPositiveMassSelfEnforcingCandidate M haccess testFeature),
      candidate.selected = selected

field maximal_active_branch_ae:
∀ {Feature : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype Feature] [inst_1 : DecidableEq Feature]
  {M : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ContinuousGaussianPopulation Feature} {haccess : 0 < M.accessLaw {true}}
  {testFeature : Feature}
  {selected :
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ReportRequiredSequentialEquilibriumData ℝ
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) ℝ}
  {selectedSource :
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ReportRequiredPositiveMassRefinedSourceEquilibrium
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianAccessPopulationLaw M)
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationBase testFeature)
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationFeature testFeature)
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationSkill selected (M.noiseVariance testFeature)},
  LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ReportRequiredFibrewiseActiveBranchSelection M haccess testFeature selected
      selectedSource →
    let law : MeasureTheory.Measure (Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) :=
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianAccessPopulationLaw M;
    let base : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ) → LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ :=
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationBase testFeature;
    let skill : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ) → ℝ := LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousPopulationSkill;
    ∀
      (candidate :
        LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ReportRequiredSourceTimedPositiveMassSelfEnforcingCandidate M haccess testFeature)
      (region : Set (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ)),
      MeasurableSet region →
        0 < law (base ⁻¹' region) →
          (∀ᵐ (student : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) ∂law.restrict (base ⁻¹' region),
              selected.takeDecision (skill student) (base student) = true →
                candidate.selected.takeDecision (skill student) (base student) = true) →
            ∀ᵐ (student : Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) ∂law.restrict (base ⁻¹' region),
              candidate.selected.takeDecision (skill student) (base student) = true →
                selected.takeDecision (skill student) (base student) = true
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

type:

                (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianPopulationLaw M)
                  (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessBaseRegionEvent testFeature region) →
              (candidate :
                  LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ReportRequiredTailCandidateData ℝ
                    (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) ℝ) →

                      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ReportRequiredTailNoReportEvent testFeature
                        candidate.takeDecision) →

value:
fun {Feature : Type u_1} [Fintype Feature] [DecidableEq Feature]
    (M : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ContinuousGaussianPopulation Feature) (testFeature : Feature)
    (region : Set (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ))
    (hregionPositive :
      0 <
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianPopulationLaw M)
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessBaseRegionEvent testFeature region))
    (candidate :
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ReportRequiredTailCandidateData ℝ
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) ℝ)
    (hnoReportPositive :
      0 <
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessLocalRawLaw M testFeature region)
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ReportRequiredTailNoReportEvent testFeature candidate.takeDecision)) =>
  have rawLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure (Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) :=
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianPopulationLaw M;
  let localLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure (Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) :=
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessLocalRawLaw M testFeature region;
  let noReportEvent : Set (Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) :=
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ReportRequiredTailNoReportEvent testFeature candidate.takeDecision;
  let actionLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure ((LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) × ℝ) :=
    MeasureTheory.Measure.map (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessBaseSkillObservation testFeature)
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21NormalizedRestriction localLaw noReportEvent);
  ∀ᵐ (publicBase :
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.Measure.map Prod.fst actionLaw,
    candidate.noReportPayoff publicBase =
      ∫ (latentSkill : ℝ), latentSkill ∂(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib Prod.snd Prod.fst actionLaw) publicBase
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

fun {Feature : Type u_1} [Fintype Feature] [DecidableEq Feature]
    (M : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ContinuousGaussianPopulation Feature) (testFeature : Feature)
    (region : Set (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ))
    (hregionPositive :
      0 <
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianPopulationLaw M)
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessBaseRegionEvent testFeature region))
    (candidate :
      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21ReportRequiredTailCandidateData ℝ
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) ℝ)
    (hreportPositive :
      0 <
        (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessLocalRawLaw M testFeature region)
          (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ReportRequiredTailReportEvent testFeature candidate.takeDecision)) =>
  have rawLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure (Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) :=
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ContinuousGaussianPopulationLaw M;
  let localLaw : MeasureTheory.Measure (Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) :=
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessLocalRawLaw M testFeature region;
  let reportEvent : Set (Bool × ℝ × (Feature → ℝ)) :=
    LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21ReportRequiredTailReportEvent testFeature candidate.takeDecision;
  let actionLaw :
    MeasureTheory.Measure ((LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) × ℝ × ℝ) :=
    MeasureTheory.Measure.map (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21HiddenAccessBaseScoreSkillObservation testFeature)
      (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21NormalizedRestriction localLaw reportEvent);
  ∀ᵐ (publicObservation :
    (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) ×
      ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.Measure.map
      (fun (scoreSkill : (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) × ℝ × ℝ) =>
        (scoreSkill.1, scoreSkill.2.1))
      actionLaw,
    candidate.reportedPayoff publicObservation.1 publicObservation.2 =
      ∫ (latentSkill : ℝ),
        latentSkill ∂(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib
            (fun (scoreSkill : (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) × ℝ × ℝ) =>
              scoreSkill.2.2)
            (fun (scoreSkill : (LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21NonTestFeature Feature testFeature → ℝ) × ℝ × ℝ) =>
              (scoreSkill.1, scoreSkill.2.1))
            actionLaw)
          publicObservation
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

\section*{Material library prerequisites}
\hypertarget{library-prerequisite-c7f802398a145c63}{}
\subsection*{\small\ttfamily\raggedright Applied\allowbreak{}Modeling\allowbreak{}Lib.\allowbreak{}Probability.\allowbreak{}Gaussian\allowbreak{}Signal\allowbreak{}Family}
\textbf{Source locator:} {\footnotesize\raggedright cited publication, lines 199-\allowbreak{}208\par}
\paragraph{Verbatim paper-source connection}
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
Students. There is a unit mass of students. Each student has a latent skill level q, normally
distributed as N (µ, σ 2 ), as well as a set of observed features Θ. All students have the ﬁrst K − 1
features {1, . . . , K − 1}; only a subset apply with the last feature K. For each student, θk is a noisy
function of q, i.e., θk = q +ǫk , for k = 1, . . . , K, with Gaussian noise ǫk ∼ N (0, σk2 ). The distribution
of noise ǫk is feature dependent, but independent across features and students. Throughout our
exposition, we refer to feature K as the “test score.”
For each student, let Z, Y, X ∈ {0, 1} be indicators denoting whether the student has access (Z)
to the test, takes (Y ) it, and reports their score (X), respectively. Of course, Z ≥ Y ≥ X. Access Z
is deterministic and pre-set, uncorrelated with q and other features. If a student has access (Z = 1),
how they make their decisions to take the test and/or report is deﬁned below.
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

% report-context-presentation-sha256: ac665fc0b4ecacfb396301fc002eba34e182dc01788fc09e1a0b8a69c41fe2f4

\end{sloppypar}

\paragraph{Exact Lean library declaration}
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
/-- Conjugate Gaussian model with a finite family of noisy signals. -/
structure GaussianSignalFamily (ι : Type*) [Fintype ι] where
  priorMean : ℝ
  priorVar : ℝ
  noiseVar : ι → ℝ
  priorVar_pos : 0 < priorVar
  noiseVar_pos : ∀ i, 0 < noiseVar i
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

\paragraph{Recorded source-to-library screening}
\textbf{Verdict:} \texttt{matches}
\\
{\raggedright
\textbf{Reason:} The finite-\allowbreak{}family interface supplies exactly the source model's prior mean, positive prior variance, and feature-\allowbreak{}indexed positive noise variances.\allowbreak{} In the graph-\allowbreak{}bound test-\allowbreak{}adjoining definition, these parameters realize the independent zero-\allowbreak{}mean Gaussian signals and the source equation theta\_\allowbreak{}k = q + epsilon\_\allowbreak{}k.\allowbreak{}
\par}
\reviewmatch{Matches source input}

fun {Skill : Type u_1} {Base : Type u_2} {Test : Type u_3} [MeasurableSpace Test]
    (requirementPolicy : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21RequirementPolicy)
    (testLaw : Skill → Base → MeasureTheory.Measure Test) (takeDecision : Skill → Base → Bool)
    (reportDecision : Base → Test → Bool) (estimatedReportedPayoff : Base → Test → ℝ)
    (estimatedWithheldPayoff estimatedNoTakePayoff : Base → ℝ)
    (estimationPolicyReportedPayoff : (Skill → Base → Bool) → (Base → Test → Bool) → Base → Test → ℝ)
    (estimationPolicyWithheldPayoff estimationPolicyNoTakePayoff :
      (Skill → Base → Bool) → (Base → Test → Bool) → Base → ℝ) =>
  (∀ (skill : Skill) (base : Base), MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure (testLaw skill base)) ∧
    (∀ (base : Base), Measurable (reportDecision base)) ∧
      (∀ (skill : Skill) (base : Base),
          MeasureTheory.Integrable
            (fun (test : Test) =>
              if reportDecision base test = true then estimatedReportedPayoff base test
              else estimatedWithheldPayoff base)
            (testLaw skill base)) ∧
        (∀ (skill : Skill) (base : Base) (test : Test),
            LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21AccessAction.feasible requirementPolicy.accessRequirement
              { takesTest := takeDecision skill base,
                reportsScore := takeDecision skill base && reportDecision base test }) ∧
          (∀ (skill : Skill) (base : Base) (alternativeTake : Bool) (alternativeReport : Test → Bool),
              (∀ (test : Test),
                  LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21AccessAction.feasible requirementPolicy.accessRequirement
                    { takesTest := alternativeTake, reportsScore := alternativeTake && alternativeReport test }) →
                Measurable alternativeReport →
                  (alternativeTake = true →
                      MeasureTheory.Integrable
                        (fun (test : Test) =>
                          if alternativeReport test = true then estimatedReportedPayoff base test
                          else estimatedWithheldPayoff base)
                        (testLaw skill base)) →
                    (if alternativeTake = true then
                        ∫ (test : Test),
                          if alternativeReport test = true then estimatedReportedPayoff base test
                          else estimatedWithheldPayoff base ∂testLaw skill base
                      else estimatedNoTakePayoff base) ≤
                      if takeDecision skill base = true then
                        ∫ (test : Test),
                          if reportDecision base test = true then estimatedReportedPayoff base test
                          else estimatedWithheldPayoff base ∂testLaw skill base
                      else estimatedNoTakePayoff base) ∧
            estimatedReportedPayoff = estimationPolicyReportedPayoff takeDecision reportDecision ∧

                estimatedNoTakePayoff = estimationPolicyNoTakePayoff takeDecision reportDecision

Definition 6 (Re-sampling policy). The sampling estimation policy, PS , is as follows.
For students with access to tests, the school uses the Bayesian optimal method to estimate the
student’s true skill level; i.e., for I , (Z = 1, {θk }K−1
k=1 , X, θK 1X ):
fPS (I) = E[q|I].
For students without access to tests, the school uses the first K − 1 features {θk }K
k=1 to generate
the distribution of the test score θK , and then randomly draws a score for each student to add to the
student’s profile. Finally, the school estimates the student’s true skill level based on the submitted
features and the randomly drawn test score; i.e., for I , (Z = 0, {θk }K−1
k=1 ):
K−1
fPS (Z = 0, {θk }K−1
k=1 ) = E[q|{θk }k=1 , θ̃K ]

where
θ̃K ∼ θK |{θk }K−1
k=1 .

\subsection*{\small\ttfamily\raggedright LG21\allowbreak{}Test\allowbreak{}Optional\allowbreak{}Policies.\allowbreak{}LG21\allowbreak{}Optional\allowbreak{}Pointwise\allowbreak{}Best\allowbreak{}Responses}
\noindent\textbf{Paper-local declaration:}\par
{\footnotesize\ttfamily\raggedright LG21\allowbreak{}Test\allowbreak{}Optional\allowbreak{}Policies.\allowbreak{}LG21\allowbreak{}Optional\allowbreak{}Pointwise\allowbreak{}Best\allowbreak{}Responses\par}
\textbf{Source locator:} {\footnotesize\raggedright cited publication, lines 229-\allowbreak{}244\par}
\paragraph{Verbatim paper-source connection}
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
Student decisions and equilibrium. Pursuant to the requirement policy, students with access
(Z = 1) may decide to take the test (Y = 1), with full knowledge of their skill q, noise distribution
ǫK , other features {θk }K−1
k=1 for k < K, and the school’s estimation policy P . Students who take the
test may, with knowledge of their test score θK , decide to report it (X = 1), in which case the school
observes their score θK . Students make these decisions to maximize the school’s estimate of their
4
K
skill level. We denote the resulting decisions via the functions Y (q, {θk }K−1
k=1 , P ) and X({θk }k=1 , P ).
We assume that the school knows the functions X(·), Y (·).5 It can then use its knowledge of
these functions to estimate the skill level of each student. For example, with Bayesian optimal
estimation, if only students with q ≥ 1 report their test score, then E[q|X = 1] ≥ 1.
Thus, for students with access, we must calculate an equilibrium: student strategies to report a
test score depend on the school’s estimation, which in turn depends on the reporting decisions of
students. An equilibrium is as follows.
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

  (∀ (base : Base),

\subsection*{\small\ttfamily\raggedright LG21\allowbreak{}Test\allowbreak{}Optional\allowbreak{}Policies.\allowbreak{}Paper\allowbreak{}Interface.\allowbreak{}distributional\_\allowbreak{}equality}
\noindent\textbf{Paper-local declaration:}\par
{\footnotesize\ttfamily\raggedright LG21\allowbreak{}Test\allowbreak{}Optional\allowbreak{}Policies.\allowbreak{}Paper\allowbreak{}Interface.\allowbreak{}distributional\_\allowbreak{}equality\par}
\textbf{Source locator:} {\footnotesize\raggedright cited publication, lines 326-\allowbreak{}335\par}
\paragraph{Verbatim paper-source connection}
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
What does test-optional mean?
The focus of this work is to understand how schools can meet their test-optional pledge. However, a prerequisite challenge is to formalize what “test-optional” even means, as it is left undeﬁned by schools beyond the idea to “not penalize students for the absence of a standardized test
score” [NACAC, 2020]. Here, we deﬁne several potential notions, on the distribution of resulting
skill estimates for students with and without test access. (Recall that, by assumption, neither true
skill distribution q nor feature distributions θk |q diﬀer by test access).
D

Our notions rely on “distributional equality,” denoted (==), because of the randomness in the
process, both in the sampling of test scores given q and in the estimation function fP . Let q̃|D, P
denote the distribution of q̃ given the information D using policy P .
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

\textbf{Verdict:} \texttt{matches}
\\
{\raggedright
\textbf{Reason:} The source introduces distributional equality as equality of the resulting law of the estimate.\allowbreak{} The exact Lean value is literal equality left = right for arbitrary law objects, which matches that notation without adding assumptions.\allowbreak{}
\par}

\subsection*{\small\ttfamily\raggedright LG21\allowbreak{}Test\allowbreak{}Optional\allowbreak{}Policies.\allowbreak{}Paper\allowbreak{}Interface.\allowbreak{}lg21\allowbreak{}Gaussian\allowbreak{}Signal\allowbreak{}Family\allowbreak{}With\allowbreak{}Test}
\noindent\textbf{Paper-local declaration:}\par
{\footnotesize\ttfamily\raggedright LG21\allowbreak{}Test\allowbreak{}Optional\allowbreak{}Policies.\allowbreak{}Paper\allowbreak{}Interface.\allowbreak{}lg21\allowbreak{}Gaussian\allowbreak{}Signal\allowbreak{}Family\allowbreak{}With\allowbreak{}Test\par}
\textbf{Source locator:} {\footnotesize\raggedright cited publication, lines 199-\allowbreak{}208\par}
\paragraph{Verbatim paper-source connection}
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
Students. There is a unit mass of students. Each student has a latent skill level q, normally
distributed as N (µ, σ 2 ), as well as a set of observed features Θ. All students have the ﬁrst K − 1
features {1, . . . , K − 1}; only a subset apply with the last feature K. For each student, θk is a noisy
function of q, i.e., θk = q +ǫk , for k = 1, . . . , K, with Gaussian noise ǫk ∼ N (0, σk2 ). The distribution
of noise ǫk is feature dependent, but independent across features and students. Throughout our
exposition, we refer to feature K as the “test score.”
For each student, let Z, Y, X ∈ {0, 1} be indicators denoting whether the student has access (Z)
to the test, takes (Y ) it, and reports their score (X), respectively. Of course, Z ≥ Y ≥ X. Access Z
is deterministic and pre-set, uncorrelated with q and other features. If a student has access (Z = 1),
how they make their decisions to take the test and/or report is deﬁned below.
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

\paragraph{Verbatim paper-source connection}
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
Definition 5. Policy P is test-blank, i.e., test scores play no role, if in every equilibrium,
D

K−1
q̃|{θk }K−1
k=1 , P == q̃|{θk }k=1 , θK , P

∀ {θk }K
k=1

Of course, test-blank policies defeat the purpose of going test-optional. In this work, we thus
ask whether it is possible to meet the fairness notions without being test-blank.
Note that these deﬁnitions all concern the estimates of the policy P in equilibrium. For example,
there are two ways for a policy to be test-blank: either the estimation function fP ignores the test
score (and whether the student reports it, X), or in equilibrium no student reports a test score. In
either case, the deﬁnition is met, and test scores play no role in student estimates. Note also that
for notational ease we suppress the requirement policy in the statement of each deﬁnition, as our
results do not depend on which requirement policy is chosen.
\end{ReviewVerbatim}



\paragraph{Lean-expanded paper semantic target}

\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
Each deﬁnition is less stringent than the previous, i.e., latent skill fairness =⇒ observable fairness =⇒ demographic fairness. The notions connect naturally to those common in the literature.
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

  (latentAccessKernel latentNoAccessKernel : S.Equilibrium → Base → ProbabilityTheory.Kernel Skill Estimate)
  (observableAccessKernel observableNoAccessKernel : S.Equilibrium → ProbabilityTheory.Kernel Base Estimate),
  (∀ (e : S.Equilibrium) (base : Base) (skill : Skill),
      (latentAccessKernel e base) skill = S.latentAccessLaw e skill base) →
    (∀ (e : S.Equilibrium) (base : Base) (skill : Skill),

Proposition 4.3. Suppose the school observes access status Z. Then, PBO – the policy that uses
Bayesian optimal estimation for all students – is not observably or demographically fair.

[Next verbatim source excerpt]


[form-feed in source extraction]
Students. There is a unit mass of students. Each student has a latent skill level q, normally
distributed as N (µ, σ 2 ), as well as a set of observed features Θ. All students have the ﬁrst K − 1
features {1, . . . , K − 1}; only a subset apply with the last feature K. For each student, θk is a noisy
function of q, i.e., θk = q +ǫk , for k = 1, . . . , K, with Gaussian noise ǫk ∼ N (0, σk2 ). The distribution
of noise ǫk is feature dependent, but independent across features and students. Throughout our
exposition, we refer to feature K as the “test score.”

[Next verbatim source excerpt]

score); if X = 1, it further includes θK . As in Garg et al. [2020], we study the Bayesian optimal
estimation policy PBO , in which the student skill estimate is the posterior mean using all the
information available:
q̃ = fPBO (I) , E[q|I].
We further consider non-Bayesian optimal estimation policies P , implemented by (potentially
randomized) functions fP .

[Next verbatim source excerpt]

Our notions rely on “distributional equality,” denoted (==), because of the randomness in the
process, both in the sampling of test scores given q and in the estimation function fP . Let q̃|D, P
denote the distribution of q̃ given the information D using policy P .

other features {θk }K−1
k=1 – with and without access are equal.
Definition 3. Policy P is observably fair if in every equilibrium, for all {θk }K−1
k=1 ,
D

K−1
q̃|Z = 1, {θk }K−1
k=1 , P == q̃|Z = 0, {θk }k=1 , P,

i.e., if the distribution of skill estimates for students – that share (observable) other features {θk }K−1
k=1
– with and without access are equal.

Definition 4. Policy P is demographically fair if in every equilibrium,

q̃|Z = 1, P == q̃|Z = 0, P,
i.e., if the distribution of skill estimates for students with and without access are equal.

[Next verbatim source excerpt]

What can the school require? We consider three requirement policies: no requirements (students
are free to take and report the test unconstrained, Z ≥ Y ≥ X); reporting required conditional
taking the test (if a student takes the test, then they must report their score, Z ≥ Y = X);3 or, in
the case where the school knows Z, reporting required given access (Z = Y = X). We ﬁnd that
such requirements do not make a diﬀerence: the same results hold for all our settings, and so we do
not include the requirement policy when denoting the school’s policy P .

k=1 , the fraction of students who have
access to tests is C ∈ (0, 1). Then we have that of this fraction of students with access, a further

\section*{Review row 6}
\textbf{Source locator:} {\footnotesize\raggedright cited publication, lines 424-\allowbreak{}450\par}
\paragraph{Verbatim source input}
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
Theorem 3.1 (Strategic withholding). Suppose the school does not know access status and uses
Bayesian optimal estimation PBO for all students. Then, some students with access do not report
test scores. In particular, in every equilibrium:
• If the school does not require reporting conditional on taking the test, then Y (·) = 1. For each
{θk }K−1
k=1 , there exists a finite test score threshold θ̄K such that an applicant reports their score,
X({θk }K−1
k=1 , θK ) = 1, if and only if θK ≥ θ̄K .
• If the school requires reporting conditional on taking the test (Y = X), then for each {θk }K−1
k=1 ,
there exists a finite skill threshold q̄ such that an applicant takes the test and reports their
score, Y ({θk }K−1
k=1 , q) = 1, if and only if q ≥ q̄.
Furthermore, PBO satisfies none of the fairness definitions.
To prove this theorem, we ﬁrst argue that neither everyone report or no one report is equilibrium.
We then show that if a fraction of the student reports their score (or takes the test), there should
be a threshold for the test score (or the skill level) for reporting (or test-taking), and then we
conclude by showing there exists such a threshold such that the equilibrium conditions hold. For
the unfairness argument, we note that in this situation the estimated skill of a portion of students
with access are guaranteed to strictly dominate those of their peers who do not have access, with
others guaranteed to weakly dominate, so any fairness conditional on test access cannot be met.
The theorem establishes that the naive school strategy – to optimally use whatever information
is provided by the student – induces students to strategically respond; as a result, the school
satisﬁes none of our fairness criteria. More generally, the proof for unfairness is independent of the
assumption that school does not know access status and the policy it uses. Instead, as long as the
strategic eﬀect exists, meeting fairness is impossible – some of the students who have access are
bound to beneﬁt from their strategic advantage.

[Next verbatim source excerpt]

Finally, to conclude that it is unfair according to all definitions, we note that in this regime,
every student who has access to test can act strategically, so that he has an estimated skill that
weakly dominates another student who is completely the same otherwise, but does not have access
to test. Furthermore, there always exist some students who have access that strictly dominate
their counterparts, otherwise the situation where no one report the score (or take the test) should
K−1
be stable. Thus, q̃|Z = 1, {θk }K−1
k=1 strictly stochastically dominates q̃|Z = 0, {θk }k=1 for every
K−1
{θk }k=1 . This means any equality in distribution that is conditional on Z cannot hold, hence all
three fairness criteria fail. As a side remark, if we were considering fairness conditioned on X, the
same argument still follows: all students with X = 1 weakly beneﬁt from this decision (and some
strictly beneﬁt), while a portion of the students with X = 0, namely those without access, does not
beneﬁt at all.

[Next verbatim source excerpt]

who would be better oﬀ if they chose to report. So no one reports is also not stable. Thus we know
that if an equilibrium exists, it must be that a fraction of the students report.

[Next verbatim source excerpt]

We can then show that neither everyone takes the test and reports nor no one takes the test
and reports are stable, so if an equilibrium exists, it must be that a fraction of students report.
Under such situation, the function Y ({θk }K−1
k=1 , q, PBO ) would be increasing with respect to q, so
that there is a threshold of q for test-taking. We can then ﬁnish by showing that there exists
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

\section*{Review row 7}
\textbf{Source locator:} {\footnotesize\raggedright cited publication, lines 424-\allowbreak{}438\par}
\paragraph{Verbatim source input}
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
Theorem 3.1 (Strategic withholding). Suppose the school does not know access status and uses
Bayesian optimal estimation PBO for all students. Then, some students with access do not report
test scores. In particular, in every equilibrium:
• If the school does not require reporting conditional on taking the test, then Y (·) = 1. For each
{θk }K−1
k=1 , there exists a finite test score threshold θ̄K such that an applicant reports their score,
X({θk }K−1
k=1 , θK ) = 1, if and only if θK ≥ θ̄K .
• If the school requires reporting conditional on taking the test (Y = X), then for each {θk }K−1
k=1 ,
there exists a finite skill threshold q̄ such that an applicant takes the test and reports their
score, Y ({θk }K−1
k=1 , q) = 1, if and only if q ≥ q̄.
Furthermore, PBO satisfies none of the fairness definitions.
To prove this theorem, we ﬁrst argue that neither everyone report or no one report is equilibrium.

[Next verbatim source excerpt]

who would be better oﬀ if they chose to report. So no one reports is also not stable. Thus we know
that if an equilibrium exists, it must be that a fraction of the students report.
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

  (∀ (skill : ℝ) (base : Base), MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure (testLaw skill base)) →
    ∀ (takeDecision : ℝ → Base → Bool) (reportDecision : Base → ℝ → Bool) (reportedPayoff : Base → ℝ → ℝ)
      (noReportPayoff : Base → ℝ),
      (∀ (skill : ℝ) (base : Base),
          MeasureTheory.Integrable
            (fun (score : ℝ) =>
              LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21OptionalSourceContinuationPayoff reportDecision reportedPayoff noReportPayoff
                base score)

        LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalPointwiseBestResponses testLaw takeDecision reportDecision reportedPayoff
            noReportPayoff →
          ∀ (scoreVariance : NNReal),
            scoreVariance ≠ 0 →
              (∀ (skill : ℝ) (base : Base), testLaw skill base = ProbabilityTheory.gaussianReal skill scoreVariance) →
                ∀ (intercept slope : Base → ℝ),
                  (∀ (base : Base), 0 < slope base) →
                    (∀ (base : Base) (score : ℝ), reportedPayoff base score = intercept base + slope base * score) →
                      (∀ (base : Base) (score : ℝ),

\section*{Review row 9}
\textbf{Source locator:} {\footnotesize\raggedright cited publication, lines 455-\allowbreak{}461\par}
\paragraph{Verbatim source input}
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
Theorem 3.2 (Fairness impossibility). Suppose the school does not know access status and uses an
estimation policy P that satisfies either latent skill or observable fairness. Then, P is test-blank.
The proof proceeds via an unraveling argument. To satisfy those fairness notions and to avoid
being test-blank, for any X(·) the policy P must randomize skill estimates to equalize the estimate distributions. However, then, some students with X({θk }K−1
k=1 , θK ) = 1 can strictly improve
their estimates by no longer reporting their score. Reporting unravels until no student reports in
equilibrium, which itself leads to the policy being test-blank.
\end{ReviewVerbatim}


\paragraph{Formalized review target}
The archival source above is not asserted equivalent to this formalized target.
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
For every supplied optional-reporting equilibrium/fibre of the approved clarified operational model, deterministic output on the reported-score branch, an arbitrary common no-report law, source fairness, and the operational Definition 5 convention imply zero reporter mass or equality of the realized output kernel and base-only law almost everywhere.
\end{ReviewVerbatim}

\textbf{Archival source anchor:} {\footnotesize\raggedright cited publication, lines 455-\allowbreak{}461\par}

      LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21OptionalOperationalTestBlank (model.scoreLaw e base)
        (model.policy.baseOnlyLaw e base) (model.reporterSet e base)

\paragraph{Recorded source-to-Spec screening}
\textbf{Verdict:} \texttt{matches}
\\
{\raggedright
\textbf{Reason:} Theorem 4.\allowbreak{}4 states that when the school observes access status, the resampling policy is observably and demographically fair.\allowbreak{} The identity-\allowbreak{}bound approved context authorizes the source-\allowbreak{}timed positive-\allowbreak{}mass equilibrium reading for the optional and report-\allowbreak{}required protocols, fibrewise maximal active-\allowbreak{}branch selection, and PBO or response obligations only on attained branches.\allowbreak{} The expanded target requires nonempty selected carriers for those two protocols, applies the common resampling fairness claim to every carrier selected under that convention, and supplies the corresponding mandatory-\allowbreak{}given-\allowbreak{}access certificate.\allowbreak{} Its LG21ObservedAccessFair dependency is exactly the conjunction of conditional-\allowbreak{}output-\allowbreak{}kernel equality and equality of the access output law with the resampled no-\allowbreak{}access output law;\allowbreak{} the Gaussian dependencies construct the same conditional resampling kernel from the shared non-\allowbreak{}test base law.\allowbreak{} Thus all three protocol instances conclude the source's observable and demographic fairness without imposing an off-\allowbreak{}path PBO or a common cross-\allowbreak{}protocol action profile.\allowbreak{}
\par}
\reviewmatch{Matches source input}

\section*{Review row 12}
\textbf{Source locator:} {\footnotesize\raggedright cited publication, lines 617-\allowbreak{}622\par}
\paragraph{Verbatim source input}
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
This procedure is akin to Thompson sampling for bandits, in which an action is played according
to the probability that it maximizes the reward. Here, if a school accepts all students with skill
estimates above some threshold q̄, then each student without access is accepted with probability
equal to the probability they would receive a high enough score to be accepted had they taken the
test; this probability is conditioned on everything the school knows about the student, {θk }K−1
k=1 .
\end{ReviewVerbatim}



\paragraph{Expanded PaperInterface specification}
\begin{ReviewVerbatim}
∀ {Omega : Type u_1} {Base : Type u_2} {Test : Type u_3} [inst : MeasurableSpace Omega] [inst_1 : MeasurableSpace Base]
  [inst_2 : MeasurableSpace Test]
  (experiment : LG21TestOptionalPolicies.LG21Definition6ExactResamplingPolicy Omega Base Test) (base : Base)
  (cutoff : ℝ),
  ((LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21Definition6ExactNoAccessEstimateKernel experiment) base) (Set.Ici cutoff) =
      (experiment.testGivenBase base) {test : Test | cutoff ≤ experiment.posteriorEstimate base test} ∧
    (((ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.id.prod experiment.testGivenBase).map fun (z : Base × Test) =>
            experiment.posteriorEstimate z.1 z.2)
          base)
        (Set.Ici cutoff) =
      ((LG21TestOptionalPolicies.lg21Definition6ExactNoAccessEstimateKernel experiment) base) (Set.Ici cutoff)
\end{ReviewVerbatim}
